RE A19 — independent held-out endgame result (072945Z). Both 2k and 8k lost W/D/L on the SAME one of 466 informative four/five-piece games; 128k lost none. 8k corrected zero 2k errors and introduced zero. Paired absolute error reduction 0.000 percentage points, conservative exact 95% [−0.936,+0.936], conditional McNemar p=1. The minimum 200 independent informative games was reached; the prespecified positive lower bound was not. On all 1113 fully classified positions, the two shallow budgets chose different moves 160 times, with identical table outcome every time. Against 128k, 2k disagreed on 296 moves (295 same W/D/L), and 8k on 245 (244 same W/D/L). More agreement with a deeper search did not demonstrate an objective endgame move improvement on these independent positions.

4096 eight-ply games, 526232 raw rows and 29092 eligible rows. The selection produced one row in 1119 games; 2977 had no eligible row. All 3357 warm replay probes agreed with their prior histories. Six positions were excluded at conservative rule50/DTZ boundaries (four root and two child). 31 included roots had a prior occurrence. Changing future repetition histories can affect claims and two drawing moves can have different practical chances. Of 1113 included full-game generator outcomes, 1075 matched the table result at the sampled root; 19 theoretically winning and 19 theoretically losing sampled roots went on to raw draws (26 rule, 12 plylimit). These are outcomes before the PC's external adjudication.

Sole error: opening ID 3101, ply153, 8/1K6/1P1kn3/8/8/8/6B1/8 b - - 6 77. Black can draw. Both warm 2k and 8k played d6c5 (−256 and −245 cp); White then has WDL +2, DTZ +9 and halfmove 7. Warm 128k played e6d8 (−229 cp), preserving the draw; e6c5 and d6e5 also draw. Returned counts were 4117, 8083 and 150550 nodes. The actual generator game was a rule draw. The six exclusions and every remaining move are in the CSV.

# Independent Forge endgame holdout

The 4,096 input IDs are the eight-ply arms of Astra's 072730Z opening study. Use the r67 net (SHA-256 737f197808fef489017cf46f4daf76663d805bb01761e2b1781fc46fa7c9fc28), the unchanged r15dsg search, all 24 commands of loop/LOOP_OPTIONS.txt, one thread and Hash 16. The Linux binary used here has SHA-256 b3870ed8f48f6a605c7766f5989ddcfa9ca0092f1036b42b59fc5827b09c8045.

1. Starting from the standard board, use Forge Board::legal_moves() in native order and C++ std::mt19937_64 seeded 202611230727 for the eight plies. For n legal moves, reject draws x < (uint64_t(-n)%n) and choose x%n. The extension uses an entirely separate PRNG 202611230728. If repetition, insufficient material, clock >=100 or no legal move occurs during the first eight, discard the incomplete prefix; stop after 4,096 complete prefixes. The TSV, including the separate ten-ply arm when available, is SHA-256 31f640c10359005a10fd365712d5cec3d4e5375e480e4930635236424e490e25. All 4,096 complete eight-ply move strings are distinct and none duplicates the original 384 strings.
2. For the eight-ply arm, send ucinewgame; isready; position startpos moves <prefix>; selfgame softnodes 2000 evallimit 2000 maxply 300 writeminply 8 drawply 80 drawscore 5 drawcount 8. Keep the raw emitted sg lines in order and the sgend. Audit code decompresses and SHA-verifies each transcript against the generating shard.
3. On each game independently filter the recorded sg FENs using only piece count in {4,5} and halfmove clock <=80. Sort by emitted order (already ordered). Choose exactly random.Random(202611230730+gameID).choice(eligible_row_indices) when the list is nonempty. The emitted FEN is checked against the reconstructed position from the actual opening and all played moves before that row.
4. For each budget 2,000, 8,000 and 128,000 separately, start a fully cleared one-thread engine via ucinewgame; isready. At each position after the randomized opening up to but excluding the selected position send position startpos moves <prefix up to this ply>; go softnodes 2000. Wait for bestmove. Require its last completed PV head, returned best move and cp score to equal the generator transcript. At the selected position use go softnodes BUDGET; store PV head/score/depth, reported final node spend. The selected 2,000 result must agree with the original move and cp; record any mismatch and exclude it from paired scoring. A FEN-only query or a fresh root search on the selected history has a different TT and heuristic history. The holdout tests only genuinely warm roots.
5. With python-chess chess 1.11.2 and local standard 3–5-piece Syzygy files, probe the actual root and every legal child. For a board, first classify checkmate as loss; then stalemate, insufficient material, actual third repetition or current 50-move draw as draw. Map five-state WDL -1,0,+1 to draw. For +/-2 use the sign when h=0, or when h+abs(DTZ)+1 <=100. Otherwise unresolved (one-ply DTZ cushion). A child value is negated to the parent's side. Exclude the whole row from paired scoring when any value is unknown, the best child differs from the root, or replay fails. An informative root has at least one inferior and one best child. The method checks immediate claims using actual history. It cannot capture future repetition claims unavailable to Syzygy; it does not distinguish practical chances between two WDL-equivalent moves.
6. Primary contrast uses independent games, one position each. For informative roots, a=2k loses a WDL class but 8k preserves, b=8k loses a WDL class but 2k preserves. Report (a-b)/n. Paired 95% interval: form joint conservative 95% bounds from two 97.5% Clopper–Pearson intervals on discordance q=(a+b)/n and correction-given-discordance p=a/(a+b); bound q(2p−1) over the rectangle. If no discordance use p=[0,1], so uncertainty remains nonzero. Require at least 200 informative games and the lower limit strictly above zero. Also report the exact two-sided conditional sign/McNemar test, raw full-game result from selected side's perspective versus best-play root, 128k move identities versus WDL, missingness and prior twofold. Raw sgend outcomes are pre external PC adjudication: the latter’s corrected training labels are unavailable for this holdout.
